I. 1. Påråj. 1. “The list of Theras is quoted in the 
Samantapåsådikå, see vol. iii. p. 313. —v. 2, Ittiyo and 
Iddbiyo АС, Ittiyo B. —Uttiyo АС, Uttiyo B. —v. 5, De- 
vatthero B, Revatth? AC. —v. 9, Puppanåmo, Pubbanåmo 
AO, Pupphanåmo, Phussanâmo B. —v. 12, Puppanåmo, 
Pubbanåmo AC, Phuesanåmo B. 

Apiyata 2. After raho В inserts paticchanne Åsane 
nålamkammeniye. 

Nissaggiya 1. Before dháresum B inserts dasåhåtikkantam. 

Nissaggiya 8 (beginning of the paragraph). After gaha- 
patikam В inserts vë gahapatánim vá. 

After the tenth Nissaggiye (at the end of the first Vagga) 
B has the following Uddåna : atirekaekarattafi ca akålaporå- 
nadhovanam patiggahai ce ќай с’ eva viññatti ca taduttarim
